```
     1→You are a clinical NLP entity extractor. Given a clinical trial eligibility criterion, extract ONLY the discrete medical concepts that should be grounded to terminology codes.
     2→
     3→## Rules
     4→1. Extract the medical ENTITY (not the full sentence). Examples:
     5→   - "eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2" -> text: "eGFR", entity_type: "Lab_Value"
     6→   - "No history of myocardial infarction" -> text: "myocardial infarction", entity_type: "Condition"
     7→   - "Currently taking calcium channel blockers" -> text: "calcium channel blockers", entity_type: "Medication"
     8→   - "Age 20 to 60 years" -> text: "age", entity_type: "Demographic"
     9→   - "Coronary artery bypass graft within 3 months" -> text: "coronary artery bypass graft", entity_type: "Procedure"
    10→
    11→2. One criterion may yield 0, 1, or multiple entities.
    12→3. Skip non-groundable concepts: consent, willingness, investigator discretion, geographic location, legal capacity.
    13→4. Entity types: Condition | Medication | Lab_Value | Procedure | Demographic | Other
    14→5. The `value` field is NOT used by this tool. Only extract `text` (the medical concept) and `entity_type`.
    15→6. Extract entities even from indication or context-framing criteria. The sentence structure does not matter — extract every medical concept regardless of how it is phrased.
    16→7. Classify clinical scoring systems, classification scales, and ordinal assessments as Lab_Value. Use "Other" only as a last resort — always prefer a specific type.
    17→
    18→## Medical Normalization Rules
    19→- Always normalize entity text to the standard medical NOUN form used in terminology systems (UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10), not adjective/adverb forms from the criterion text. Adjective forms produce poor terminology search results:
    20→  - "immunocompromised" → "immunodeficiency" (Condition)
    21→  - "hypertensive" → "hypertension" (Condition)
    22→  - "febrile" → "fever" (Condition)
    23→- When a criterion describes a state resulting from a medical intervention, extract the INTERVENTION as a Procedure — not the resulting state as a Condition. In clinical trial eligibility language, these criteria require proof of a past procedure:
    24→  - "immunized against hepatitis B" → "hepatitis B vaccination" (Procedure)
    25→  - "must have undergone appendectomy" → "appendectomy" (Procedure)
    26→  - Prefer "-tion"/"-ectomy"/"-plasty" procedure nouns over "-ity"/"-ness" state nouns, as procedure nouns match terminology systems better and avoid ambiguity.
    27→- Do NOT embed demographic qualifiers (male/female/age) into clinical entity text — those belong as separate Demographic entities. Mixing demographics into clinical terms confuses terminology search:
    28→  - "Male patients with renal failure" → "renal failure" (Condition) AND "male" (Demographic)
    29→  - "Elderly subjects with osteoporosis" → "osteoporosis" (Condition) AND "age" (Demographic)
    30→
    31→## Compound Concept Rules — DO NOT Over-Decompose
    32→- Do NOT separate anatomical modifiers, severities, laterality, or procedure types from their parent concept. These are a single medical entity:
    33→  - "tricompartmental knee replacement" → ONE entity (not "knee replacement" + "tricompartmental")
    34→  - "spinal muscular atrophy" → ONE entity
    35→  - "non-small cell lung cancer" → ONE entity
    36→  - "left ventricular hypertrophy" → ONE entity
    37→  - "chronic obstructive pulmonary disease" → ONE entity
    38→  - "bilateral total hip arthroplasty" → ONE entity
    39→- DO separate distinct comorbidities or co-occurring conditions into individual entities:
    40→  - "Type 2 diabetes with nephropathy" → TWO entities: "type 2 diabetes" AND "nephropathy"
    41→  - "Hypertension and heart failure" → TWO entities: "hypertension" AND "heart failure"
    42→  - "Asthma or COPD" → TWO entities: "asthma" AND "chronic obstructive pulmonary disease"
    43→
    44→## Criterion
    45→{% if category %}Category hint: {{ category }}{% endif %}
    46→Text: "{{ criterion_text }}"
    47→
    48→Return a JSON object with key "entities" containing an array of {"text": string, "entity_type": string} objects.
    49→If no groundable entities exist, return {"entities": []}.
    50→
```

```
     1→"""Ground node: route entities via TerminologyRouter and select with MedGemma.
     2→
     3→This node is the fourth in the protocol processing pipeline:
     4→ingest→extract→parse→ground→persist
     5→It implements the delegation pattern: thin orchestration that calls tools.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Error accumulation: entity failures are logged and accumulated, not fatal
     9→- Audit trail: all grounding decisions logged to AuditLog
    10→- TerminologyRouter handles entity-type-aware API routing
    11→- MedGemma selects the best match from candidates
    12→- Field mappings generated per entity for AutoCriteria decomposition
    13→- Consent entities skipped explicitly (not groundable to medical codes)
    14→- Demographics routed normally via umls/snomed (age/gender agentic reasoning)
    15→- Agentic retry loop: max 3 attempts, expert_review routing on exhaustion
    16→
    17→Architecture note: Graph nodes ARE allowed to import from api-service for DB access.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import asyncio
    23→import json
    24→import logging
    25→import os
    26→import re
    27→import time
    28→from typing import Any
    29→
    30→from api_service.storage import engine
    31→from rapidfuzz import fuzz
    32→from shared.models import AuditLog
    33→from sqlmodel import Session
    34→
    35→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    36→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    37→from protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import generate_field_mappings
    38→from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
    39→    agentic_reasoning_loop,
    40→    medgemma_decide,
    41→)
    42→from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import (
    43→    OmopLookupResult,
    44→    _get_omop_engine,
    45→    lookup_omop_concept,
    46→)
    47→from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import (
    48→    TerminologyRouter,
    49→    _is_likely_acronym,
    50→)
    51→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import normalize_value
    52→
    53→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    54→
    55→# Singleton router instance (loads YAML config once)
    56→_router: TerminologyRouter | None = None
    57→
    58→
    59→def _get_router() -> TerminologyRouter:
    60→    """Get or create the singleton TerminologyRouter."""
    61→    global _router  # noqa: PLW0603
    62→    if _router is None:
    63→        _router = TerminologyRouter()
    64→    return _router
    65→
    66→
    67→def _reconcile_dual_grounding(
    68→    result: EntityGroundingResult,
    69→    omop_result: OmopLookupResult,
    70→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    71→    """Reconcile TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper results.
    72→
    73→    Sets omop_concept_id and reconciliation_status on the result:
    74→    - Both succeed → "agree" (high confidence) or "disagreement"
    75→    - Only TerminologyRouter → "omop_missing"
    76→    - Only OMOP → "tooluniverse_missing"
    77→    - Neither → no change (existing expert_review behavior)
    78→
    79→    Agreement is checked by comparing the OMOP concept_name against
    80→    the ToolUniverse preferred_term (fuzzy match).
    81→
    82→    Args:
    83→        result: EntityGroundingResult from TerminologyRouter path.
    84→        omop_result: OmopLookupResult from OMOP mapper path.
    85→
    86→    Returns:
    87→        The same EntityGroundingResult, mutated with OMOP fields.
    88→    """
    89→    tu_ok = result.selected_code is not None
    90→    omop_ok = omop_result.omop_concept_id is not None
    91→
    92→    if tu_ok and omop_ok:
    93→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
    94→        # Fuzzy agreement check using token_set_ratio (order-insensitive)
    95→        tu_term = (result.preferred_term or "").lower()
    96→        omop_term = (omop_result.omop_concept_name or "").lower()
    97→        if tu_term and omop_term and fuzz.token_set_ratio(tu_term, omop_term) >= 80:
    98→            result.reconciliation_status = "agree"
    99→        else:
   100→            result.reconciliation_status = "disagreement"
```

```
     1→"""Parse node: persist CriteriaBatch and Criteria records from extraction JSON.
     2→
     3→This node:
     4→1. Parses extraction_json into ExtractionResult Pydantic model
     5→2. Creates CriteriaBatch and Criteria DB records
     6→3. Builds entities_json for the ground node
     7→4. Clears pdf_bytes from state (size optimization)
     8→
     9→IMPORTANT: This node does NOT publish a CriteriaExtracted outbox event.
    10→Per PIPE-03: criteria_extracted outbox removed. The pipeline continues
    11→directly to the ground node in a single unified pass.
    12→
    13→Architecture note: Graph nodes are integration glue and ARE allowed to import
    14→from api-service for database access. Cross-service imports are intentional
    15→for workflow orchestration nodes.
    16→"""
    17→
    18→from __future__ import annotations
    19→
    20→import asyncio
    21→import json
    22→import logging
    23→import os
    24→from typing import Any
    25→
    26→from api_service.storage import engine
    27→from shared.models import Criteria, CriteriaBatch, Protocol

    30→from protocol_processor.schemas.extraction import ExtractionResult
    31→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    32→from protocol_processor.tools.entity_decomposer import decompose_entities_from_criterion
    33→from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
    34→
    35→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    36→
    37→
    38→async def parse_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    39→    """Persist extraction results to DB and prepare entities for grounding.
    40→
    41→    Parses extraction_json, creates CriteriaBatch and Criteria records,
    42→    then builds entities_json containing the data needed by the ground node.
    43→    Clears pdf_bytes from state after extraction to reduce state size.
    44→
    45→    Args:
    46→        state: Current pipeline state with extraction_json and protocol_id.
    47→
    48→    Returns:
    49→        Dict with batch_id, entities_json, and pdf_bytes=None,
    50→        or error dict on failure.
    51→    """
    52→    if state.get("error"):
    53→        return {}
    54→
    55→    with pipeline_span("parse_node", protocol_id=state.get("protocol_id", "")) as span:
    56→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": state.get("protocol_id", "")})
    57→
    58→        try:
    59→            extraction_json = state.get("extraction_json")
    60→            if not extraction_json:
    61→                span.set_outputs({"error": "No extraction_json in state"})
    62→                return {
    63→                    "error": "No extraction_json in state"
    64→                    " — extract node may have failed"
    65→                }
    66→
    67→            # Parse extraction JSON into Pydantic model
    68→            extraction_result = ExtractionResult.model_validate_json(extraction_json)
    69→
    70→            # Dev/debug: truncate criteria for faster runs and fewer API calls.
    71→            # DEV_MAX_CRITERIA takes precedence over PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA when set.
    72→            _max_criteria = int(
    73→                os.getenv("DEV_MAX_CRITERIA")
    74→                or os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA")
    75→                or "0"
    76→            )
    77→            if _max_criteria > 0:
    78→                extraction_result.criteria = extraction_result.criteria[:_max_criteria]
    79→                logger.info(
    80→                    "Max criteria limit=%d: truncated to %d criteria",
    81→                    _max_criteria,
    82→                    len(extraction_result.criteria),
    83→                )
    84→
    85→            extraction_model = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
    86→
    87→            # Phase A: Persist criteria to DB and collect raw data
    88→            criteria_raws: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    89→            criterion_ids: list[str] = []
    90→
    91→            with Session(engine) as session:
    92→                # Create CriteriaBatch
    93→                batch = CriteriaBatch(
    94→                    protocol_id=state["protocol_id"],
    95→                    status="pending_review",
    96→                    extraction_model=extraction_model,
    97→                )
    98→                session.add(batch)
    99→                session.flush()  # Get batch.id
   100→
   101→                # Create Criteria records and collect raw data for decomposition
   102→                for extracted in extraction_result.criteria:
   103→                    raw = extracted.model_dump()
   104→                    criterion = Criteria(
   105→                        batch_id=batch.id,
   106→                        criteria_type=raw["criteria_type"],
   107→                        category=raw.get("category"),
   108→                        text=raw["text"],
   109→                        temporal_constraint=raw.get("temporal_constraint"),
   110→                        conditions=raw.get("conditions"),
   111→                        numeric_thresholds=raw.get("numeric_thresholds"),
   112→                        assertion_status=raw.get("assertion_status"),
   113→                        confidence=raw.get("confidence", 1.0),
   114→                        source_section=raw.get("source_section"),
   115→                        page_number=raw.get("page_number"),
   116→                    )
   117→                    session.add(criterion)
   118→                    session.flush()  # Get criterion.id
   119→
   120→                    criteria_raws.append(raw)
   121→                    criterion_ids.append(criterion.id)
   122→
   123→                # Update protocol status to 'processing' (not 'extracted' — grounding
   124→                # happens in the same pipeline, full status set by persist node)
   125→                protocol = session.get(Protocol, state["protocol_id"])
   126→                if protocol:
   127→                    protocol.status = "grounding"
   128→                    session.add(protocol)
   129→
   130→                session.commit()
   131→
   132→                # Capture batch_id before session closes (avoids DetachedInstanceError)
   133→                batch_id = batch.id
   134→
   135→            # Phase B: Decompose criteria into discrete medical entities concurrently
   136→            # Runs OUTSIDE DB session — async LLM calls shouldn't hold sessions open
   137→            decompose_tasks = [
   138→                decompose_entities_from_criterion(raw["text"], raw.get("category"))
   139→                for raw in criteria_raws
   140→            ]
   141→            decomposed_results = await asyncio.gather(*decompose_tasks)
   142→
   143→            # Build entity_items from decomposition results
   144→            entity_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   145→            for raw, cid, decomposed in zip(
   146→                criteria_raws, criterion_ids, decomposed_results
   147→            ):
   148→                if decomposed:
   149→                    for ent in decomposed:
   150→                        entity_items.append(
   151→                            {
   152→                                "criterion_id": cid,
   153→                                "text": ent["text"],
   154→                                "entity_type": ent["entity_type"],
   155→                                "criterion_text": raw["text"],
   156→                                "criteria_type": raw["criteria_type"],
   157→                            }
   158→                        )
   159→                else:
   160→                    # Fallback: category-based type mapping with full text
   161→                    category = raw.get("category", "")
   162→                    fallback_type = {
   163→                        "medications": "Medication",
   164→                        "lab_values": "Lab_Value",
   165→                        "procedures": "Procedure",
   166→                        "demographics": "Demographic",
   167→                    }.get(category, "Condition")
   168→                    entity_items.append(
   169→                        {
   170→                            "criterion_id": cid,
   171→                            "text": raw["text"],
   172→                            "entity_type": fallback_type,
   173→                            "criterion_text": raw["text"],
   174→                            "criteria_type": raw["criteria_type"],
   175→                        }
   176→                    )
   177→
   178→            logger.info(
   179→                "Parsed CriteriaBatch %s: %d criteria -> %d entities for protocol %s",
   180→                batch_id,
   181→                len(criteria_raws),
   182→                len(entity_items),
   183→                state["protocol_id"],
   184→            )
   185→
   186→            # Build entities_json for the ground node
   187→            entities_json = json.dumps(entity_items)
   188→
   189→            span.set_outputs(
   190→                {
   191→                    "batch_id": batch_id,
   192→                    "criteria_count": len(entity_items),
   193→                }
   194→            )
   195→
   196→            # Return batch_id, entities_json; clear pdf_bytes (state size optimization)
   197→            return {
   198→                "batch_id": batch_id,
   199→                "entities_json": entities_json,
   200→                "pdf_bytes": None,  # No longer needed after extraction
   201→            }
   202→
   203→        except Exception as e:
   204→            logger.exception(
   205→                "Parse failed for protocol %s: %s",
   206→                state.get("protocol_id", "unknown"),
   207→                e,
   208→            )
   209→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   210→            return {"error": f"Parse failed: {e}"}
   211→
```

> AGENT

Here's the diagnosis:

**Root cause: Entity decomposition returned 0 entities for the two simple age criteria.** This is a Gemini flakiness issue, not caused by my changes.

The flow:
1. `parse_node` calls `decompose_entities_from_criterion("Patients over 89 years old", "other")` 
2. Gemini returned `{"entities": []}` — empty
3. The fallback (line 159-176 of `parse.py`) kicks in: it uses the full text `"Patients over 89 years old"` with type `"Condition"` (because category `"other"` defaults to `"Condition"`)
4. Grounding then tries to ground `"Patients over 89 years old"` as a Condition — which fails because it's not a condition, it's a demographic criterion
5. Result: zero entities, zero field mappings

**Why "Patients at least 18 years of age, and less than 89 years old" works**: Gemini successfully decomposed it → extracted `age` as `Demographic` → grounded to `C0001779`.

**The fix is in the fallback logic.** When category is `"other"` and decomposition fails, it defaults to `"Condition"`, but these are age criteria. Two options:

1. **Quick fix**: Change the fallback to detect age-related text and assign `"Demographic"` type
2. **Better fix**: Retry entity decomposition once before falling back, or use a regex heuristic for age patterns

Which approach do you prefer?

> DEVELOPER

{

```json
  "description": "Find processing traces (not API calls)"
}
```

```
     1→"""Entity decomposition: extract discrete medical entities from criteria.
     2→
     3→Criterion sentences like "eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2 or sCr <= 2.0 mg/dL" contain
     4→multiple groundable medical concepts. This tool decomposes them into discrete
     5→entities with correct types for TerminologyRouter dispatch.
     6→
     7→Uses the two-model architecture pattern: Gemini for structured output via
     8→LangChain's with_structured_output, matching the medgemma_decider.py pattern.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from pathlib import Path
    16→from typing import Literal, cast
    17→
    18→from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader
    19→from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    20→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→PROMPTS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "prompts"
    25→
    26→# Unicode math symbols that Gemini/PDF extraction may produce.
    27→# Normalize to ASCII equivalents so the LLM template handles them cleanly.
    28→_UNICODE_OPERATORS: dict[str, str] = {
    29→    "\u2265": ">=",  # ≥
    30→    "\u2264": "<=",  # ≤
    31→    "\u2260": "!=",  # ≠
    32→    "\u00b1": "+-",  # ±
    33→    "\u2013": "-",  # en-dash
    34→    "\u2014": "-",  # em-dash
    35→}
    36→
    37→
    38→def _normalize_criterion_text(text: str) -> str:
    39→    """Replace unicode math/comparison symbols with ASCII equivalents."""
    40→    for char, repl in _UNICODE_OPERATORS.items():
    41→        text = text.replace(char, repl)
    42→    return text
    43→
    44→
    45→class DecomposedEntity(BaseModel):
    46→    """A single medical entity extracted from a criterion sentence.
    47→
    48→    Attributes:
    49→        text: The specific medical term to ground (e.g. "eGFR", not the full sentence).
    50→        entity_type: Entity type matching routing.yaml keys exactly.
    51→    """
    52→
    53→    text: str = Field(description="The specific medical term to ground")
    54→    entity_type: Literal[
    55→        "Condition", "Medication", "Lab_Value", "Procedure", "Demographic", "Other"
    56→    ] = Field(description="Entity type for terminology routing")
    57→
    58→
    59→class DecomposedEntityList(BaseModel):
    60→    """List of decomposed entities from a single criterion sentence."""
    61→
    62→    entities: list[DecomposedEntity] = Field(default_factory=list)
    63→
    64→
    65→def _render_decompose_prompt(criterion_text: str, category: str | None) -> str:
    66→    """Render the entity decomposition Jinja2 prompt template.
    67→
    68→    Args:
    69→        criterion_text: Full criterion sentence to decompose.
    70→        category: Optional category hint from extraction (e.g. "lab_values").
    71→
    72→    Returns:
    73→        Rendered prompt string for Gemini.
    74→    """
    75→    env = Environment(loader=FileSystemLoader(str(PROMPTS_DIR)), autoescape=False)
    76→    template = env.get_template("entity_decompose.jinja2")
    77→    return template.render(criterion_text=criterion_text, category=category or "")
    78→
    79→
    80→async def decompose_entities_from_criterion(
    81→    criterion_text: str,
    82→    category: str | None,
    83→) -> list[dict]:
    84→    """Extract discrete medical entities from a criterion sentence.
    85→
    86→    Uses Gemini with structured output to decompose a criterion sentence
    87→    into individual medical terms with correct entity types. Falls back
    88→    to empty list on failure -- caller handles fallback to full-text entity.
    89→
    90→    Args:
    91→        criterion_text: The full criterion sentence to decompose.
    92→        category: Optional category hint from extraction.
    93→
    94→    Returns:
    95→        List of dicts with "text" and "entity_type" keys, or empty list on failure.
    96→    """
    97→    # Normalize unicode operators (≥, ≤, etc.) to ASCII before LLM call
    98→    normalized_text = _normalize_criterion_text(criterion_text)
    99→
   100→    try:
   101→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   102→            model=os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash"),
   103→            google_api_key=os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
   104→        )
   105→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   106→
   107→        model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   108→        structured = gemini.with_structured_output(DecomposedEntityList)
   109→        prompt = _render_decompose_prompt(normalized_text, category)
   110→
   111→        with llm_span("gemini_entity_decompose", model_name) as llm:
   112→            llm.set_request(prompt)
   113→            result = await structured.ainvoke(prompt)
   114→            llm.set_response(str(result))
   115→
   116→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   117→            result = DecomposedEntityList.model_validate(result)
   118→        decomposed = cast(DecomposedEntityList, result)
   119→
   120→        if not decomposed.entities:
   121→            logger.warning(
   122→                "Entity decomposition returned 0 entities for criterion '%s'",
   123→                normalized_text[:80],
   124→            )
   125→
   126→        return [e.model_dump() for e in decomposed.entities]
   127→    except Exception as e:
   128→        logger.error(
   129→            "Entity decomposition failed for criterion '%s': %s",
   130→            normalized_text[:80],
   131→            e,
   132→            exc_info=True,
   133→        )
   134→        return []  # Caller falls back to full-text entity
   135→
```

```
     1→"""Parse node: persist CriteriaBatch and Criteria records from extraction JSON.
     2→
     3→This node:
     4→1. Parses extraction_json into ExtractionResult Pydantic model
     5→2. Creates CriteriaBatch and Criteria DB records
     6→3. Builds entities_json for the ground node
     7→4. Clears pdf_bytes from state (size optimization)
     8→
     9→IMPORTANT: This node does NOT publish a CriteriaExtracted outbox event.
    10→Per PIPE-03: criteria_extracted outbox removed. The pipeline continues
    11→directly to the ground node in a single unified pass.
    12→
    13→Architecture note: Graph nodes are integration glue and ARE allowed to import
    14→from api-service for database access. Cross-service imports are intentional
    15→for workflow orchestration nodes.
    16→"""
    17→
    18→from __future__ import annotations
    19→
    20→import asyncio
    21→import json
    22→import logging
    23→import os
    24→from typing import Any
    25→
    26→from api_service.storage import engine
    27→from shared.models import Criteria, CriteriaBatch, Protocol
    28→from sqlmodel import Session
    29→
    30→from protocol_processor.schemas.extraction import ExtractionResult
    31→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    32→from protocol_processor.tools.entity_decomposer import decompose_entities_from_criterion
    33→from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
    34→
    35→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    36→
    37→
    38→async def parse_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    39→    """Persist extraction results to DB and prepare entities for grounding.
    40→
    41→    Parses extraction_json, creates CriteriaBatch and Criteria records,
    42→    then builds entities_json containing the data needed by the ground node.
    43→    Clears pdf_bytes from state after extraction to reduce state size.
    44→
    45→    Args:
    46→        state: Current pipeline state with extraction_json and protocol_id.
    47→
    48→    Returns:
    49→        Dict with batch_id, entities_json, and pdf_bytes=None,
    50→        or error dict on failure.
    51→    """
    52→    if state.get("error"):
    53→        return {}
    54→
    55→    with pipeline_span("parse_node", protocol_id=state.get("protocol_id", "")) as span:
    56→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": state.get("protocol_id", "")})
    57→
    58→        try:
    59→            extraction_json = state.get("extraction_json")
    60→            if not extraction_json:
    61→                span.set_outputs({"error": "No extraction_json in state"})
    62→                return {
    63→                    "error": "No extraction_json in state"
    64→                    " — extract node may have failed"
    65→                }
    66→
    67→            # Parse extraction JSON into Pydantic model
    68→            extraction_result = ExtractionResult.model_validate_json(extraction_json)
    69→
    70→            # Dev/debug: truncate criteria for faster runs and fewer API calls.
    71→            # DEV_MAX_CRITERIA takes precedence over PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA when set.
    72→            _max_criteria = int(
    73→                os.getenv("DEV_MAX_CRITERIA")
    74→                or os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_CRITERIA")
    75→                or "0"
    76→            )
    77→            if _max_criteria > 0:
    78→                extraction_result.criteria = extraction_result.criteria[:_max_criteria]
    79→                logger.info(
    80→                    "Max criteria limit=%d: truncated to %d criteria",
    81→                    _max_criteria,
    82→                    len(extraction_result.criteria),
    83→                )
    84→
    85→            extraction_model = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
    86→
    87→            # Phase A: Persist criteria to DB and collect raw data
    88→            criteria_raws: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    89→            criterion_ids: list[str] = []
    90→
    91→            with Session(engine) as session:
    92→                # Create CriteriaBatch
    93→                batch = CriteriaBatch(
    94→                    protocol_id=state["protocol_id"],
    95→                    status="pending_review",
    96→                    extraction_model=extraction_model,
    97→                )
    98→                session.add(batch)
    99→                session.flush()  # Get batch.id
   100→
   101→                # Create Criteria records and collect raw data for decomposition
   102→                for extracted in extraction_result.criteria:
   103→                    raw = extracted.model_dump()
   104→                    criterion = Criteria(
   105→                        batch_id=batch.id,
   106→                        criteria_type=raw["criteria_type"],
   107→                        category=raw.get("category"),
   108→                        text=raw["text"],
   109→                        temporal_constraint=raw.get("temporal_constraint"),
   110→                        conditions=raw.get("conditions"),
   111→                        numeric_thresholds=raw.get("numeric_thresholds"),
   112→                        assertion_status=raw.get("assertion_status"),
   113→                        confidence=raw.get("confidence", 1.0),
   114→                        source_section=raw.get("source_section"),
   115→                        page_number=raw.get("page_number"),
   116→                    )
   117→                    session.add(criterion)
   118→                    session.flush()  # Get criterion.id
   119→
   120→                    criteria_raws.append(raw)
   121→                    criterion_ids.append(criterion.id)
   122→
   123→                # Update protocol status to 'processing' (not 'extracted' — grounding
   124→                # happens in the same pipeline, full status set by persist node)
   125→                protocol = session.get(Protocol, state["protocol_id"])
   126→                if protocol:
   127→                    protocol.status = "grounding"
   128→                    session.add(protocol)
   129→
   130→                session.commit()
   131→
   132→                # Capture batch_id before session closes (avoids DetachedInstanceError)
   133→                batch_id = batch.id
   134→
   135→            # Phase B: Decompose criteria into discrete medical entities concurrently
   136→            # Runs OUTSIDE DB session — async LLM calls shouldn't hold sessions open
   137→            decompose_tasks = [
   138→                decompose_entities_from_criterion(raw["text"], raw.get("category"))
   139→                for raw in criteria_raws
   140→            ]
   141→            decomposed_results = await asyncio.gather(*decompose_tasks)
   142→
   143→            # Build entity_items from decomposition results
   144→            entity_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   145→            for raw, cid, decomposed in zip(
   146→                criteria_raws, criterion_ids, decomposed_results
   147→            ):
   148→                if decomposed:
   149→                    for ent in decomposed:
   150→                        entity_items.append(
   151→                            {
   152→                                "criterion_id": cid,
   153→                                "text": ent["text"],
   154→                                "entity_type": ent["entity_type"],
   155→                                "criterion_text": raw["text"],
   156→                                "criteria_type": raw["criteria_type"],
   157→                            }
   158→                        )
   159→                else:
   160→                    # Fallback: category-based type mapping with full text
   161→                    category = raw.get("category", "")
   162→                    fallback_type = {
   163→                        "medications": "Medication",
   164→                        "lab_values": "Lab_Value",
   165→                        "procedures": "Procedure",
   166→                        "demographics": "Demographic",
   167→                    }.get(category, "Condition")
   168→                    entity_items.append(
   169→                        {
   170→                            "criterion_id": cid,
   171→                            "text": raw["text"],
   172→                            "entity_type": fallback_type,
   173→                            "criterion_text": raw["text"],
   174→                            "criteria_type": raw["criteria_type"],
   175→                        }
   176→                    )
   177→
   178→            logger.info(
   179→                "Parsed CriteriaBatch %s: %d criteria -> %d entities for protocol %s",
   180→                batch_id,
   181→                len(criteria_raws),
   182→                len(entity_items),
   183→                state["protocol_id"],
   184→            )
   185→
   186→            # Build entities_json for the ground node
   187→            entities_json = json.dumps(entity_items)
   188→
   189→            span.set_outputs(
   190→                {
   191→                    "batch_id": batch_id,
   192→                    "criteria_count": len(entity_items),
   193→                }
   194→            )
   195→
   196→            # Return batch_id, entities_json; clear pdf_bytes (state size optimization)
   197→            return {
   198→                "batch_id": batch_id,
   199→                "entities_json": entities_json,
   200→                "pdf_bytes": None,  # No longer needed after extraction
   201→            }
   202→
   203→        except Exception as e:
   204→            logger.exception(
   205→                "Parse failed for protocol %s: %s",
   206→                state.get("protocol_id", "unknown"),
   207→                e,
   208→            )
   209→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   210→            return {"error": f"Parse failed: {e}"}
   211→
```

```
     1→You are a clinical NLP entity extractor. Given a clinical trial eligibility criterion, extract ONLY the discrete medical concepts that should be grounded to terminology codes.
     2→
     3→## Rules
     4→1. Extract the medical ENTITY (not the full sentence). Examples:
     5→   - "eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2" -> text: "eGFR", entity_type: "Lab_Value"
     6→   - "No history of myocardial infarction" -> text: "myocardial infarction", entity_type: "Condition"
     7→   - "Currently taking calcium channel blockers" -> text: "calcium channel blockers", entity_type: "Medication"
     8→   - "Age 20 to 60 years" -> text: "age", entity_type: "Demographic"
     9→   - "Coronary artery bypass graft within 3 months" -> text: "coronary artery bypass graft", entity_type: "Procedure"
    10→
    11→2. One criterion may yield 0, 1, or multiple entities.
    12→3. Skip non-groundable concepts: consent, willingness, investigator discretion, geographic location, legal capacity.
    13→4. Entity types: Condition | Medication | Lab_Value | Procedure | Demographic | Other
    14→5. The `value` field is NOT used by this tool. Only extract `text` (the medical concept) and `entity_type`.
    15→6. Extract entities even from indication or context-framing criteria. The sentence structure does not matter — extract every medical concept regardless of how it is phrased.
    16→7. Classify clinical scoring systems, classification scales, and ordinal assessments as Lab_Value. Use "Other" only as a last resort — always prefer a specific type.
    17→
    18→## Medical Normalization Rules
    19→- Always normalize entity text to the standard medical NOUN form used in terminology systems (UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10), not adjective/adverb forms from the criterion text. Adjective forms produce poor terminology search results:
    20→  - "immunocompromised" → "immunodeficiency" (Condition)
    21→  - "hypertensive" → "hypertension" (Condition)
    22→  - "febrile" → "fever" (Condition)
    23→- When a criterion describes a state resulting from a medical intervention, extract the INTERVENTION as a Procedure — not the resulting state as a Condition. In clinical trial eligibility language, these criteria require proof of a past procedure:
    24→  - "immunized against hepatitis B" → "hepatitis B vaccination" (Procedure)
    25→  - "must have undergone appendectomy" → "appendectomy" (Procedure)
    26→  - Prefer "-tion"/"-ectomy"/"-plasty" procedure nouns over "-ity"/"-ness" state nouns, as procedure nouns match terminology systems better and avoid ambiguity.
    27→- Do NOT embed demographic qualifiers (male/female/age) into clinical entity text — those belong as separate Demographic entities. Mixing demographics into clinical terms confuses terminology search:
    28→  - "Male patients with renal failure" → "renal failure" (Condition) AND "male" (Demographic)
    29→  - "Elderly subjects with osteoporosis" → "osteoporosis" (Condition) AND "age" (Demographic)
    30→
    31→## Compound Concept Rules — DO NOT Over-Decompose
    32→- Do NOT separate anatomical modifiers, severities, laterality, or procedure types from their parent concept. These are a single medical entity:
    33→  - "tricompartmental knee replacement" → ONE entity (not "knee replacement" + "tricompartmental")
    34→  - "spinal muscular atrophy" → ONE entity
    35→  - "non-small cell lung cancer" → ONE entity
    36→  - "left ventricular hypertrophy" → ONE entity
    37→  - "chronic obstructive pulmonary disease" → ONE entity
    38→  - "bilateral total hip arthroplasty" → ONE entity
    39→- DO separate distinct comorbidities or co-occurring conditions into individual entities:
    40→  - "Type 2 diabetes with nephropathy" → TWO entities: "type 2 diabetes" AND "nephropathy"
    41→  - "Hypertension and heart failure" → TWO entities: "hypertension" AND "heart failure"
    42→  - "Asthma or COPD" → TWO entities: "asthma" AND "chronic obstructive pulmonary disease"
    43→
    44→## Criterion
    45→{% if category %}Category hint: {{ category }}{% endif %}
    46→Text: "{{ criterion_text }}"
    47→
    48→Return a JSON object with key "entities" containing an array of {"text": string, "entity_type": string} objects.
    49→If no groundable entities exist, return {"entities": []}.
    50→
```

```
    90→    Args:

    96→    """

    98→    normalized_text = _normalize_criterion_text(criterion_text)
    99→
   100→    try:
   101→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   102→            model=os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash"),

   106→

   109→        prompt = _render_decompose_prompt(normalized_text, category)
   110→
   111→        with llm_span("gemini_entity_decompose", model_name) as llm:
   112→            llm.set_request(prompt)

   115→
   116→        if isinstance(result, dict):

   131→            e,
```

```
   168→                "Entity decomposition returned 0 entities after %d retries for '%s'",
   169→                max_retries, normalized_text[:80],
   170→            )
   171→
   172→        return [e.model_dump() for e in decomposed.entities]
   173→    except Exception as e:
   174→        logger.error(
   175→            "Entity decomposition failed for criterion '%s': %s",
   176→            normalized_text[:80],
   177→            e,
   178→            exc_info=True,
   179→        )
   180→        return []
   181→
```

> AGENT

Both files pass syntax check. Here's a summary of the changes:

**`entity_decomposer.py`** — Added retry-with-rewording logic:
- New `_rephrase_for_retry()` function that wraps the criterion text with slightly different framing:
  - Retry 1: `"Eligibility criterion: {text}. Identify every medical concept."`
  - Retry 2: `"Clinical trial requirement — {text}. What medical entities are mentioned?"`
- New `_invoke_decompose()` helper that does a single LLM call with tracing
- `decompose_entities_from_criterion()` now makes the original attempt, then if 0 entities are returned, retries up to 2 times with rephrased wording before giving up

**`parse.py`** — Removed blind fallback:
- The old code fell back to typing the entire criterion text as "Condition" when decomposition returned empty — this was the root cause of age criteria failing (full sentence grounded as "Condition" fails)
- Now it just logs a warning and skips the criterion, since the decomposer already retried with wording variations

The protocol processor service should pick up these changes via `--reload`. You can test by re-uploading a protocol with simple age criteria like "Patients over 89 years old".

> DEVELOPER

Maybe instead of skipping the criterion we should ask medgemma?

> AGENT

Good idea. When Gemini decomposition fails even after retries, we can ask MedGemma to identify the medical entity — it's a medical expert and should handle simple clinical concepts like "age" easily.

Let me see how MedGemma is called elsewhere in the codebase.

```
     1→"""MedGemma decision tool for best-match selection from TerminologyRouter candidates.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "MedGemma acts as decision-maker, minimum token usage."
     4→MedGemma evaluates candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and selects the best
     5→match for each entity. Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical
     6→reasoning, Gemini for structured output parsing.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→import os

    14→
    15→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    16→    from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import TerminologyRouter

    33→def _get_prompts_dir():

    38→
    39→def _render_template(template_name: str, **kwargs: Any) -> str:
    40→    """Render a Jinja2 template from the prompts directory."""
    41→    from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader
    42→
    43→    prompts_dir = _get_prompts_dir()
    44→    env = Environment(loader=FileSystemLoader(str(prompts_dir)), autoescape=False)
    45→    template = env.get_template(template_name)
    46→    return template.render(**kwargs)
    47→

    49→class AgenticReasoningResult(BaseModel):
    50→    """MedGemma's 3-question reasoning output for failed grounding retry.
    51→
    52→    Used by agentic_reasoning_loop to determine whether to skip an entity,
    53→    apply derived entity mapping, or rephrase the query for a better search.
    54→
    55→    Attributes:
    56→        should_skip: True if entity is not a valid medical criterion.
    57→        is_derived: True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept.
    58→        derived_term: Standard concept term if is_derived is True.
    59→        rephrased_query: Rephrased medical terminology query if applicable.
    60→        gemini_suggestion: Optional additional reformulation from Gemini.
    61→        reasoning: Brief explanation of the reasoning decisions.
    62→    """
    63→
    64→    should_skip: bool = Field(
    65→        default=False,
    66→        description=(
    67→            "True if entity is not a valid medical criterion "
    68→            "(e.g., consent, participation, willingness)"
    69→        ),
    70→    )
    71→    is_derived: bool = Field(
    72→        default=False,
    73→        description="True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept",
    74→    )
    75→    derived_term: str | None = Field(
    76→        default=None,
    77→        description=(
    78→            "Standard concept term if is_derived is True "
    79→            "(e.g., 'age' for 'age >= 18 years')"
    80→        ),
    81→    )
    82→    rephrased_query: str | None = Field(
    83→        default=None,
    84→        description=(
    85→            "Rephrased medical terminology query for better search "
    86→            "(e.g., 'hypertension' for 'high blood pressure')"
    87→        ),
    88→    )
    89→    gemini_suggestion: str | None = Field(
    90→        default=None,
    91→        description=("Optional reformulation suggestion from Gemini structuring step"),

    93→    reasoning: str = Field(

    99→class GroundingDecision(BaseModel):
   100→    """MedGemma's decision for the best terminology match."""
   101→
   102→    selected_code: str | None = Field(

   125→        ),

   127→    reasoning: str = Field(

   130→    )

   133→def _get_medgemma_model() -> Any:
   134→    """Get or create MedGemma model instance."""
   135→    global _model_loader  # noqa: PLW0603
   136→    if _model_loader is None:
   137→        from inference.config import AgentConfig
   138→        from inference.model_garden import create_model_loader
   139→
   140→        _model_loader = create_model_loader(AgentConfig.from_env())
   141→    return _model_loader()
   142→
   143→
   144→async def _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> GroundingDecision:
   145→    """Structure raw MedGemma output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   146→
   147→    Args:
   148→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma output (free-form medical reasoning).
   149→
   150→    Returns:
   151→        GroundingDecision with selected code and confidence.
   152→    """
   153→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   154→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   155→
   156→    if not google_api_key:
   157→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   158→
   159→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   160→
   161→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   162→        model=gemini_model_name,
   163→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   164→    )
   165→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(GroundingDecision)
   166→
   167→    prompt = (
   168→        "Extract the grounding decision from this medical terminology analysis."
   169→        " Return the selected code, system, preferred term, confidence,"
   170→        f" and reasoning.\n\n{raw_text}"
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_decision", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   174→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   175→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   176→        resp_text = str(result) if not isinstance(result, dict) else str(result)
   177→        llm.set_response(resp_text)
   178→
   179→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   180→        return GroundingDecision.model_validate(result)
   181→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   182→
   183→
   184→async def medgemma_decide(
   185→    entity: dict,
   186→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate],
   187→    criterion_context: str,
   188→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   189→    """Use MedGemma to select the best terminology match for an entity.
   190→
   191→    MedGemma evaluates the candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and
   192→    selects the most appropriate code for the entity. Returns a result with
   193→    confidence=0.0 and no code if no candidates are available or none are
   194→    appropriate.
   195→
   196→    Args:
   197→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   198→        candidates: List of GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   199→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   200→
   201→    Returns:
   202→        EntityGroundingResult with the selected code, confidence, and reasoning.
   203→    """
   204→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   205→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   206→    criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   207→
   208→    # If no candidates, return result with confidence=0.0
   209→    if not candidates:
   210→        logger.info(
   211→            "No candidates for entity '%s' (type=%s, criterion=%s)"
   212→            " — returning empty grounding",
   213→            entity_text[:50],
   214→            entity_type,
   215→            criterion_id[:12],
   216→        )
   217→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   218→            entity_text=entity_text,
   219→            entity_type=entity_type,
   220→            selected_code=None,
   221→            selected_system=None,
   222→            preferred_term=None,
   223→            confidence=0.0,
   224→            candidates=[],
   225→            reasoning="No terminology candidates available from any API",
   226→        )
   227→
   228→    try:
   229→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   230→
   231→        system_prompt = _render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   232→        evaluate_prompt = _render_template(
   233→            "grounding_evaluate.jinja2",
   234→            entity_text=entity_text,
   235→            entity_type=entity_type,
   236→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   237→            candidates=candidates,
   238→        )
   239→
   240→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   241→
   242→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   243→        messages = [
   244→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   245→            HumanMessage(content=evaluate_prompt),
   246→        ]
   247→
   248→        with llm_span("medgemma_evaluate", str(model_name)) as llm:
   249→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {evaluate_prompt}")
   250→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   251→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   252→            usage: dict[str, int] = {}
   253→            um = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   254→            if isinstance(um, dict):
   255→                if um.get("input_tokens"):
   256→                    usage["input_tokens"] = um["input_tokens"]
   257→                if um.get("output_tokens"):
   258→                    usage["output_tokens"] = um["output_tokens"]
   259→                if um.get("total_tokens"):
   260→                    usage["total_tokens"] = um["total_tokens"]
   261→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage or None)
   262→
   263→        logger.debug(
   264→            "MedGemma evaluate response for '%s' (first 200 chars): %s",
   265→            entity_text[:30],
   266→            raw_text[:200],
   267→        )
   268→
   269→        decision = await _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text)
   270→
   271→        logger.info(
   272→            "Grounding decision for '%s': code=%s, system=%s, conf=%.2f",
   273→            entity_text[:50],
   274→            decision.selected_code,
   275→            decision.selected_system,
   276→            decision.confidence,
   277→        )
   278→
   279→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   280→            entity_text=entity_text,
   281→            entity_type=entity_type,
   282→            selected_code=decision.selected_code,
   283→            selected_system=decision.selected_system,
   284→            preferred_term=decision.preferred_term,
   285→            confidence=decision.confidence,
   286→            candidates=candidates,
   287→            reasoning=decision.reasoning,
   288→        )
   289→
   290→    except Exception as e:
   291→        logger.error(
   292→            "MedGemma decision failed for entity '%s': %s",
   293→            entity_text[:50],
   294→            e,
   295→            exc_info=True,
   296→        )
   297→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   298→            entity_text=entity_text,
   299→            entity_type=entity_type,
   300→            selected_code=None,
   301→            selected_system=None,
   302→            preferred_term=None,
   303→            confidence=0.0,
   304→            candidates=candidates,
   305→            reasoning=f"MedGemma decision failed: {e}",
   306→        )
   307→
   308→
   309→async def _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   310→    """Structure raw MedGemma reasoning output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   311→
   312→    Gemini also acts as a collaborating agent here — it can add its own
   313→    reformulation suggestions via the gemini_suggestion field when structuring
   314→    the output.
   315→
   316→    Args:
   317→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma reasoning output (free-form text).
   318→
   319→    Returns:
   320→        AgenticReasoningResult with structured 3-question answers.
   321→    """
   322→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   323→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   324→
   325→    if not google_api_key:
   326→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   327→
   328→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   329→
   330→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   331→        model=gemini_model_name,
   332→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   333→    )
   334→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(AgenticReasoningResult)
   335→
   336→    prompt = (
   337→        "Extract the structured reasoning from this medical entity analysis. "
   338→        "Determine: (1) should_skip if entity is not a valid medical criterion, "
   339→        "(2) is_derived and derived_term if entity maps to a standard concept, "
   340→        "(3) rephrased_query if a better medical term would improve search. "
   341→        "You may also add a gemini_suggestion with your own reformulation if "
   342→        "you can improve on the analysis.\n\n"
   343→        f"{raw_text}"
   344→    )
   345→
   346→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_reasoning", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   347→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   348→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   349→        llm.set_response(str(result))
   350→
   351→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   352→        return AgenticReasoningResult.model_validate(result)
   353→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   354→
   355→
   356→async def agentic_reasoning_loop(
   357→    entity: dict,
   358→    criterion_context: str,
   359→    router: "TerminologyRouter",
   360→    attempt: int = 1,
   361→) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   362→    """Ask MedGemma 3 reasoning questions to determine retry strategy.
   363→
   364→    Called by ground_node when an entity fails initial grounding (zero
   365→    candidates or confidence < 0.5). Asks MedGemma in a single prompt:
   366→    - Q1: Is this a valid medical criterion (or should it be skipped)?
   367→    - Q2: Is this a derived entity that maps to a standard concept?
   368→    - Q3: Can this entity be rephrased for better terminology search?
   369→
   370→    Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical reasoning, Gemini
   371→    for structured output parsing (and optional reformulation suggestion).
   372→
   373→    Args:
   374→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   375→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   376→        router: TerminologyRouter instance (for get_apis_for_entity context).
   377→        attempt: Current attempt number (1-3) for prompt context.
   378→
   379→    Returns:
   380→        AgenticReasoningResult with should_skip, is_derived, derived_term,
   381→        rephrased_query, gemini_suggestion, and reasoning fields.
   382→        On error, returns default result (no skip, no rephrase) to allow
   383→        the retry loop to continue with the original query.
   384→    """
   385→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   386→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   387→    previous_query = entity.get("_previous_query", entity_text)
   388→
   389→    logger.info(
   390→        "Agentic reasoning (attempt %d) for entity '%s' (type=%s)",
   391→        attempt,
   392→        entity_text[:50],
   393→        entity_type,
   394→    )
   395→
   396→    try:
   397→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   398→
   399→        system_prompt = _render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   400→        reasoning_prompt = _render_template(
   401→            "grounding_reasoning.jinja2",
   402→            entity_text=entity_text,
   403→            entity_type=entity_type,
   404→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   405→            previous_query=previous_query,
   406→            attempt=attempt,
   407→        )
   408→
   409→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   410→
   411→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   412→        messages = [
   413→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   414→            HumanMessage(content=reasoning_prompt),
   415→        ]
   416→
   417→        with llm_span("medgemma_reasoning", str(model_name)) as llm:
   418→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {reasoning_prompt}")
   419→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   420→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   421→            usage_r: dict[str, int] = {}
   422→            um_r = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   423→            if isinstance(um_r, dict):
   424→                if um_r.get("input_tokens"):
   425→                    usage_r["input_tokens"] = um_r["input_tokens"]
   426→                if um_r.get("output_tokens"):
   427→                    usage_r["output_tokens"] = um_r["output_tokens"]
   428→                if um_r.get("total_tokens"):
   429→                    usage_r["total_tokens"] = um_r["total_tokens"]
   430→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage_r or None)
   431→
   432→        logger.debug(
   433→            "MedGemma reasoning response for '%s' (first 300 chars): %s",
   434→            entity_text[:30],
   435→            raw_text[:300],
   436→        )
   437→
   438→        result = await _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text)
   439→
   440→        logger.info(
   441→            "Agentic reasoning result for '%s': skip=%s, derived=%s, "
   442→            "derived_term=%s, rephrased=%s",
   443→            entity_text[:50],
   444→            result.should_skip,
   445→            result.is_derived,
   446→            result.derived_term,
   447→            result.rephrased_query,
   448→        )
   449→
   450→        return result
   451→
   452→    except Exception as e:
   453→        logger.error(
   454→            "Agentic reasoning loop failed for entity '%s': %s",
   455→            entity_text[:50],
   456→            e,
   457→            exc_info=True,
   458→        )
   459→        # Return default result (no skip, no rephrase) — retry with original query
   460→        return AgenticReasoningResult(
   461→            should_skip=False,
   462→            is_derived=False,
   463→            derived_term=None,
   464→            rephrased_query=None,
   465→            reasoning=f"Reasoning loop failed: {e}",
   466→        )
   467→
```
