     1→"""MedGemma model loading for Vertex AI and local GPU backends.
     2→
     3→Provides two deployment paths for MedGemma (google/medgemma-4b-it):
     4→
     5→1. **Vertex AI Model Garden** (``MODEL_BACKEND=vertex``):
     6→   Uses ``ModelGardenChatModel``, a LangChain BaseChatModel wrapper that
     7→   calls a Vertex AI endpoint with Gemma chat template formatting and
     8→   exponential-backoff retry for transient errors.
     9→
    10→2. **Local GPU** (``MODEL_BACKEND=local``):
    11→   Uses ``LocalMedGemmaChatModel``, which loads the HuggingFace model
    12→   locally with optional 4-bit/8-bit quantization via bitsandbytes.
    13→   Requires an NVIDIA GPU with 8 GB+ VRAM for the default 4-bit config.
    14→
    15→Both backends expose a standard LangChain ``BaseChatModel`` interface so
    16→the rest of the pipeline (``medgemma_decider.py``, ``ground.py``) is
    17→backend-agnostic.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import logging
    23→import time
    24→from typing import Any, Callable
    25→
    26→import requests  # type: ignore[import-untyped]
    27→from langchain_core.callbacks.manager import CallbackManagerForLLMRun
    28→from langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import BaseChatModel
    29→from langchain_core.messages import AIMessage, BaseMessage
    30→from langchain_core.outputs import ChatGeneration, ChatResult
    31→from pydantic import Field
    32→from shared.lazy_cache import lazy_singleton
    33→from tenacity import (
    34→    retry,
    35→    retry_if_exception,
    36→    stop_after_attempt,
    37→    wait_exponential,
    38→)
    39→
    40→from inference.config import AgentConfig
    41→
    42→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    43→
    44→
    45→def _build_gemma_prompt(messages: list[Any]) -> str:
    46→    """Build Gemma chat template prompt from messages.
    47→
    48→    Args:
    49→        messages: List of LangChain messages.
    50→
    51→    Returns:
    52→        Formatted prompt string.
    53→    """
    54→    prompt_parts = []
    55→    for msg in messages:
    56→        role = "user"
    57→        if msg.type == "ai":
    58→            role = "model"
    59→
    60→        content = msg.content
    61→        if not isinstance(content, str):
    62→            content = str(content)
    63→
    64→        prompt_parts.append(f"<start_of_turn>{role}\n{content}<end_of_turn>")
    65→
    66→    return "\n".join(prompt_parts) + "\n<start_of_turn>model\n"
    67→
    68→
    69→def _strip_model_garden_artifacts(text: str, full_prompt: str) -> str:
    70→    """Strip echoed prompts and end-of-turn markers from response.
    71→
    72→    Note: This is now minimal because Gemini handles JSON structuring.
    73→    MedGemma's output goes to Gemini, so thinking tokens and other artifacts
    74→    are handled by Gemini's structured output parser.
    75→    """
    76→    # Strip echoed prompt if present
    77→    if text.startswith(full_prompt):
    78→        text = text[len(full_prompt) :].strip()
    79→
    80→    # Strip trailing end-of-turn marker
    81→    if text.endswith("<end_of_turn>"):
    82→        text = text[: -len("<end_of_turn>")].strip()
    83→
    84→    return text
    85→
    86→
    87→def _is_retryable_error(exception: BaseException) -> bool:
    88→    """Check if an exception is retryable (transient error).
    89→
    90→    Args:
    91→        exception: The exception to check.
    92→
    93→    Returns:
    94→        True if the exception is retryable, False otherwise.
    95→    """
    96→    # Retry on network/connection errors
    97→    if isinstance(
    98→        exception,
    99→        (
   100→            requests.exceptions.ConnectionError,
   101→            requests.exceptions.Timeout,
   102→            requests.exceptions.ChunkedEncodingError,
   103→        ),
   104→    ):
   105→        return True
   106→
   107→    # Retry on Google API transient server errors
   108→    try:
   109→        from google.api_core import exceptions as google_exceptions
   110→
   111→        if isinstance(
   112→            exception,
   113→            (
   114→                google_exceptions.ServiceUnavailable,
   115→                google_exceptions.InternalServerError,
   116→                google_exceptions.DeadlineExceeded,
   117→                google_exceptions.ResourceExhausted,
   118→            ),
   119→        ):
   120→            return True
   121→
   122→        # Do NOT retry on client errors
   123→        if isinstance(
   124→            exception,
   125→            (
   126→                google_exceptions.PermissionDenied,
   127→                google_exceptions.Unauthenticated,
   128→                google_exceptions.InvalidArgument,
   129→                google_exceptions.NotFound,
   130→                google_exceptions.AlreadyExists,
   131→                google_exceptions.FailedPrecondition,
   132→                google_exceptions.OutOfRange,
   133→            ),
   134→        ):
   135→            return False
   136→    except ImportError:
   137→        pass  # google-api-core not installed; skip Google-specific checks
   138→
   139→    # Do NOT retry on expired credentials — no point retrying with the same creds
   140→    try:
   141→        from google.auth.exceptions import RefreshError
   142→
   143→        if isinstance(exception, RefreshError):
   144→            return False
   145→    except ImportError:
   146→        pass  # google-auth not installed; skip RefreshError check
   147→
   148→    return False
   149→
   150→
   151→@retry(
   152→    retry=retry_if_exception(_is_retryable_error),
   153→    stop=stop_after_attempt(3),
   154→    wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, min=1, max=10),
   155→    reraise=True,
   156→)
   157→def _predict_with_retry(
   158→    endpoint: Any, instances: list[dict[str, Any]], parameters: dict[str, Any]
   159→) -> Any:
   160→    """Call Vertex AI endpoint.predict with retry on transient errors.
   161→
   162→    Args:
   163→        endpoint: Vertex AI Endpoint object.
   164→        instances: List of prediction instances.
   165→        parameters: Prediction parameters.
   166→
   167→    Returns:
   168→        Prediction response from the endpoint.
   169→    """
   170→    return endpoint.predict(instances=instances, parameters=parameters)
   171→
   172→
   173→class ModelGardenChatModel(BaseChatModel):
   174→    """LangChain ChatModel wrapper for Vertex AI Model Garden endpoints.
   175→
   176→    Wraps a Vertex AI endpoint that serves MedGemma (or other Gemma-family
   177→    models) using the Gemma chat template for prompt formatting and
   178→    exponential-backoff retry for transient errors.
   179→
   180→    The Endpoint object is instantiated once in the constructor and reused
   181→    across calls to enable gRPC channel reuse and avoid per-call overhead.
   182→    """
   183→
   184→    endpoint_resource_name: str
   185→    project: str
   186→    location: str
   187→    max_output_tokens: int = Field(default=512)
   188→    _endpoint: Any = None
   189→
   190→    def model_post_init(self, __context: Any) -> None:
   191→        """Initialize the Vertex AI Endpoint once for connection reuse."""
   192→        from google.cloud import aiplatform
   193→
   194→        self._endpoint = aiplatform.Endpoint(self.endpoint_resource_name)
   195→
   196→    def _generate(
   197→        self,
   198→        messages: list[BaseMessage],
   199→        stop: list[str] | None = None,
   200→        run_manager: CallbackManagerForLLMRun | None = None,
   201→        **kwargs: Any,
   202→    ) -> ChatResult:
   203→        """Generate a chat response from the Model Garden endpoint.
   204→
   205→        Args:
   206→            messages: List of LangChain messages.
   207→            stop: Optional stop sequences (unused by Model Garden).
   208→            run_manager: Optional callback manager.
   209→            **kwargs: Additional keyword arguments (e.g. temperature).
   210→
   211→        Returns:
   212→            ChatResult containing the model's response.
   213→        """
   214→        endpoint = self._endpoint
   215→
   216→        full_prompt = _build_gemma_prompt(messages)
   217→
   218→        instance = {
   219→            "prompt": full_prompt,
   220→            "max_tokens": self.max_output_tokens,
   221→            "temperature": kwargs.get("temperature", 0.1),
   222→            "top_p": 0.95,
   223→            "top_k": 40,
   224→        }
   225→        parameters = {
   226→            "max_output_tokens": self.max_output_tokens,
   227→            "temperature": kwargs.get("temperature", 0.1),
   228→        }
   229→
   230→        start_time = time.time()
   231→        try:
   232→            logger.debug(
   233→                "Calling Vertex AI endpoint.predict for endpoint: %s",
   234→                self.endpoint_resource_name,
   235→            )
   236→            response = _predict_with_retry(
   237→                endpoint=endpoint, instances=[instance], parameters=parameters
   238→            )
   239→            duration = time.time() - start_time
   240→            logger.debug(
   241→                "Vertex AI endpoint.predict succeeded in %.2f seconds",
   242→                duration,
   243→            )
   244→        except Exception as e:
   245→            duration = time.time() - start_time
   246→            logger.warning(
   247→                "Vertex AI endpoint.predict failed after %.2f seconds: %s",
   248→                duration,
   249→                e,
   250→                exc_info=True,
   251→            )
   252→            raise
   253→
   254→        text = response.predictions[0]
   255→        text = _strip_model_garden_artifacts(text, full_prompt)
   256→
   257→        message = AIMessage(content=text)
   258→        generation = ChatGeneration(message=message)
   259→        return ChatResult(generations=[generation])
   260→
   261→    @property
   262→    def _llm_type(self) -> str:
   263→        return "vertex_model_garden"
   264→
   265→
   266→def _validate_vertex_config(cfg: AgentConfig) -> tuple[str, str, str, str]:
   267→    """Validate Vertex AI configuration fields.
   268→
   269→    Args:
   270→        cfg: AgentConfig instance.
   271→
   272→    Returns:
   273→        Tuple of (project_id, region, endpoint_id, vertex_model_name).
   274→
   275→    Raises:
   276→        ValueError: If required configuration is missing.
   277→    """
   278→    project_id = (cfg.gcp_project_id or "").strip()
   279→    region = (cfg.gcp_region or "").strip()
   280→    endpoint_id = (cfg.vertex_endpoint_id or "").strip()
   281→    vertex_model_name = (cfg.vertex_model_name or "").strip()
   282→
   283→    if not project_id:
   284→        raise ValueError("GCP_PROJECT_ID is required when MODEL_BACKEND=vertex")
   285→    if not region:
   286→        raise ValueError("GCP_REGION is required when MODEL_BACKEND=vertex")
   287→    if not endpoint_id and not vertex_model_name:
   288→        raise ValueError(
   289→            "VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID or VERTEX_MODEL_NAME is required when "
   290→            "MODEL_BACKEND=vertex"
   291→        )
   292→
   293→    return project_id, region, endpoint_id, vertex_model_name
   294→
   295→
   296→class LocalMedGemmaChatModel(BaseChatModel):
   297→    """LangChain ChatModel wrapper for locally-loaded MedGemma.
   298→
   299→    Loads google/medgemma-4b-it (or a custom model path) via HuggingFace
   300→    ``transformers`` with optional bitsandbytes quantization. The model
   301→    and tokenizer are loaded once on first call and reused.
   302→
   303→    Requires:
   304→        - NVIDIA GPU with sufficient VRAM (8 GB+ for 4-bit, 16 GB+ for 8-bit)
   305→        - ``torch``, ``transformers``, ``accelerate`` packages
   306→        - ``bitsandbytes`` package (for quantized loading)
   307→    """
   308→
   309→    model_path: str = Field(default="google/medgemma-4b-it")
   310→    quantization: str = Field(default="4bit")
   311→    max_output_tokens: int = Field(default=512)
   312→    _model: Any = None
   313→    _tokenizer: Any = None
   314→
   315→    def model_post_init(self, __context: Any) -> None:
   316→        """Validate that required packages are available."""
   317→        pass  # Defer actual loading to first call for fast startup
   318→
   319→    def _ensure_loaded(self) -> None:
   320→        """Load model and tokenizer on first use."""
   321→        if self._model is not None:
   322→            return
   323→
   324→        try:
   325→            import torch
   326→            from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
   327→        except ImportError as exc:
   328→            raise ImportError(
   329→                "Local MedGemma backend requires torch, transformers, and "
   330→                "accelerate installed. Install with: "
   331→                "pip install torch transformers accelerate bitsandbytes"
   332→            ) from exc
   333→
   334→        if not torch.cuda.is_available():
   335→            raise RuntimeError(
   336→                "Local MedGemma backend requires an NVIDIA GPU with CUDA. "
   337→                "No CUDA device detected. Use MODEL_BACKEND=vertex for "
   338→                "cloud-based inference instead."
   339→            )
   340→
   341→        logger.info(
   342→            "Loading MedGemma locally: model=%s, quantization=%s",
   343→            self.model_path,
   344→            self.quantization,
   345→        )
   346→
   347→        model_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
   348→            "device_map": "auto",
   349→            "torch_dtype": torch.bfloat16,
   350→        }
   351→
   352→        if self.quantization in ("4bit", "8bit"):
   353→            try:
   354→                from transformers import BitsAndBytesConfig
   355→            except ImportError as exc:
   356→                raise ImportError(
   357→                    "Quantized loading requires bitsandbytes. "
   358→                    "Install with: pip install bitsandbytes"
   359→                ) from exc
   360→
   361→            if self.quantization == "4bit":
   362→                model_kwargs["quantization_config"] = BitsAndBytesConfig(
   363→                    load_in_4bit=True,
   364→                    bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.bfloat16,
   365→                    bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,
   366→                    bnb_4bit_quant_type="nf4",
   367→                )
   368→            else:
   369→                model_kwargs["quantization_config"] = BitsAndBytesConfig(
   370→                    load_in_8bit=True,
   371→                )
   372→
   373→        start_time = time.time()
   374→        self._tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(self.model_path)
   375→        self._model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
   376→            self.model_path, **model_kwargs
   377→        )
   378→        duration = time.time() - start_time
   379→        logger.info("MedGemma loaded in %.1f seconds", duration)
   380→
   381→    def _generate(
   382→        self,
   383→        messages: list[BaseMessage],
   384→        stop: list[str] | None = None,
   385→        run_manager: CallbackManagerForLLMRun | None = None,
   386→        **kwargs: Any,
   387→    ) -> ChatResult:
   388→        """Generate a response using the locally-loaded MedGemma model.
   389→
   390→        Args:
   391→            messages: List of LangChain messages.
   392→            stop: Optional stop sequences.
   393→            run_manager: Optional callback manager.
   394→            **kwargs: Additional keyword arguments (e.g. temperature).
   395→
   396→        Returns:
   397→            ChatResult containing the model's response.
   398→        """
   399→        import torch
   400→
   401→        self._ensure_loaded()
   402→
   403→        full_prompt = _build_gemma_prompt(messages)
   404→
   405→        inputs = self._tokenizer(full_prompt, return_tensors="pt").to(
   406→            self._model.device
   407→        )
   408→        input_length = inputs["input_ids"].shape[1]
   409→
   410→        start_time = time.time()
   411→        with torch.no_grad():
   412→            outputs = self._model.generate(
   413→                **inputs,
   414→                max_new_tokens=self.max_output_tokens,
   415→                temperature=kwargs.get("temperature", 0.1),
   416→                top_p=0.95,
   417→                top_k=40,
   418→                do_sample=True,
   419→            )
   420→        duration = time.time() - start_time
   421→
   422→        # Decode only the generated tokens (exclude prompt)
   423→        generated_tokens = outputs[0][input_length:]
   424→        text = self._tokenizer.decode(generated_tokens, skip_special_tokens=True)
   425→        text = _strip_model_garden_artifacts(text, full_prompt)
   426→
   427→        logger.debug(
   428→            "Local MedGemma generated %d tokens in %.2f seconds",
   429→            len(generated_tokens),
   430→            duration,
   431→        )
   432→
   433→        message = AIMessage(content=text)
   434→        generation = ChatGeneration(message=message)
   435→        return ChatResult(generations=[generation])
   436→
   437→    @property
   438→    def _llm_type(self) -> str:
   439→        return "local_medgemma"
   440→
   441→
   442→def create_model_loader(config: AgentConfig | None = None) -> Callable[[], Any]:
   443→    """Create a lazy MedGemma model loader for the configured backend.
   444→
   445→    Supports two backends:
   446→        - ``vertex``: Vertex AI Model Garden (cloud, recommended for production)
   447→        - ``local``: Local GPU with HuggingFace transformers (requires NVIDIA GPU)
   448→
   449→    Args:
   450→        config: Agent configuration. Defaults to ``AgentConfig.from_env()``.
   451→
   452→    Returns:
   453→        Callable that loads and returns a LangChain chat model when invoked.
   454→
   455→    Raises:
   456→        ValueError: If required configuration is missing for the selected backend.
   457→    """
   458→    cfg = config or AgentConfig.from_env()
   459→
   460→    if cfg.backend == "vertex":
   461→        return _create_vertex_model_loader(cfg)
   462→
   463→    if cfg.backend == "ollama":
   464→        return _create_ollama_model_loader(cfg)
   465→
   466→    return _create_local_model_loader(cfg)
   467→
   468→
   469→def _create_vertex_model_loader(cfg: AgentConfig) -> Callable[[], Any]:
   470→    """Create a lazy Vertex AI model loader.
   471→
   472→    Args:
   473→        cfg: Validated AgentConfig with backend=="vertex".
   474→
   475→    Returns:
   476→        Callable that lazily initializes and returns the model.
   477→    """
   478→    project_id, region, endpoint_id, vertex_model_name = _validate_vertex_config(cfg)
   479→
   480→    @lazy_singleton
   481→    def load_model() -> Any:
   482→        try:
   483→            import vertexai
   484→        except ImportError as exc:
   485→            raise ImportError(
   486→                "Vertex AI backend requires google-cloud-aiplatform installed."
   487→            ) from exc
   488→
   489→        vertexai.init(project=project_id, location=region)
   490→
   491→        if vertex_model_name:
   492→            try:
   493→                from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
   494→            except ImportError as exc:
   495→                raise ImportError(
   496→                    "Vertex AI backend requires langchain-google-genai installed."
   497→                ) from exc
   498→
   499→            return ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   500→                model=vertex_model_name,
   501→                project=project_id,
   502→                location=region,
   503→                vertexai=True,
   504→                max_output_tokens=cfg.max_new_tokens,
   505→            )
   506→
   507→        endpoint_resource_name = (
   508→            f"projects/{project_id}/locations/{region}/endpoints/{endpoint_id}"
   509→        )
   510→        return ModelGardenChatModel(
   511→            endpoint_resource_name=endpoint_resource_name,
   512→            project=project_id,
   513→            location=region,
   514→            max_output_tokens=cfg.max_new_tokens,
   515→        )
   516→
   517→    return load_model
   518→
   519→
   520→def _create_ollama_model_loader(cfg: AgentConfig) -> Callable[[], Any]:
   521→    """Create a lazy Ollama model loader via LangChain ChatOllama.
   522→
   523→    Args:
   524→        cfg: AgentConfig with backend=="ollama".
   525→
   526→    Returns:
   527→        Callable that lazily initializes and returns the model.
   528→    """
   529→
   530→    @lazy_singleton
   531→    def load_model() -> Any:
   532→        try:
   533→            from langchain_community.chat_models import ChatOllama
   534→        except ImportError as exc:
   535→            raise ImportError(
   536→                "Ollama backend requires langchain-community installed. "
   537→                "Install with: pip install langchain-community"
   538→            ) from exc
   539→
   540→        logger.info(
   541→            "Initializing Ollama model: base_url=%s, model=%s",
   542→            cfg.ollama_base_url,
   543→            cfg.ollama_model,
   544→        )
   545→        return ChatOllama(
   546→            base_url=cfg.ollama_base_url,
   547→            model=cfg.ollama_model,
   548→            temperature=0.1,
   549→        )
   550→
   551→    return load_model
   552→
   553→
   554→def _create_local_model_loader(cfg: AgentConfig) -> Callable[[], Any]:
   555→    """Create a lazy local MedGemma model loader.
   556→
   557→    Loads MedGemma (default: google/medgemma-4b-it) from HuggingFace
   558→    with optional 4-bit or 8-bit quantization via bitsandbytes.
   559→
   560→    Args:
   561→        cfg: AgentConfig with backend=="local".
   562→
   563→    Returns:
   564→        Callable that lazily initializes and returns the model.
   565→    """
   566→
   567→    @lazy_singleton
   568→    def load_model() -> Any:
   569→        logger.info(
   570→            "Initializing local MedGemma: model=%s, quantization=%s",
   571→            cfg.model_path,
   572→            cfg.quantization,
   573→        )
   574→        return LocalMedGemmaChatModel(
   575→            model_path=cfg.model_path,
   576→            quantization=cfg.quantization,
   577→            max_output_tokens=cfg.max_new_tokens,
   578→        )
   579→
   580→    return load_model
   581→

{

  "old_string": "        if not decomposed.entities:\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Entity decomposition returned 0 entities after %d retries for '%s'\",\n                max_retries, normalized_text[:80],\n            )\n\n        return [e.model_dump() for e in decomposed.entities]\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(\n            \"Entity decomposition failed for criterion '%s': %s\",\n            normalized_text[:80],\n            e,\n            exc_info=True,\n        )\n        return []",
  "new_string": "        if not decomposed.entities:\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Entity decomposition returned 0 entities after %d retries for '%s'\",\n                max_retries, normalized_text[:80],\n            )\n\n        return [e.model_dump() for e in decomposed.entities]\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(\n            \"Entity decomposition failed for criterion '%s': %s\",\n            normalized_text[:80],\n            e,\n            exc_info=True,\n        )\n        return []\n\n\nasync def medgemma_decompose_entities(\n    criterion_text: str,\n) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Fallback: ask MedGemma to identify medical entities when Gemini fails.\n\n    Uses MedGemma (medical expert) to extract entity names, then Gemini to\n    structure the output into typed entities. Called by parse_node when\n    Gemini decomposition returns empty even after retries.\n\n    Args:\n        criterion_text: The criterion sentence to decompose.\n\n    Returns:\n        List of dicts with \"text\" and \"entity_type\" keys, or empty list on failure.\n    \"\"\"\n    normalized_text = _normalize_criterion_text(criterion_text)\n\n    try:\n        from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage\n\n        from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import _get_medgemma_model\n        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span\n\n        model = _get_medgemma_model()\n        model_name = getattr(model, \"model_name\", \"\") or getattr(model, \"model\", \"\")\n\n        messages = [\n            SystemMessage(\n                content=(\n                    \"You are a medical terminology expert. Given a clinical trial \"\n                    \"eligibility criterion, list every discrete medical concept that \"\n                    \"should be coded in a terminology system (UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10). \"\n                    \"For each concept, state the concept name and whether it is a \"\n                    \"Condition, Medication, Lab_Value, Procedure, Demographic, or Other.\"\n                )\n            ),\n            HumanMessage(\n                content=f'Criterion: \"{normalized_text}\"\\n\\nList the medical entities.'\n            ),\n        ]\n\n        with llm_span(\"medgemma_entity_decompose\", str(model_name)) as llm:\n            llm.set_request(str(messages))\n            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)\n            raw_text = raw_response.content\n            llm.set_response(str(raw_text))\n\n        # Use Gemini to structure MedGemma's free-text into typed entities\n        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(\n            model=os.getenv(\"GEMINI_MODEL_NAME\", \"gemini-2.5-flash\"),\n            google_api_key=os.getenv(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY\"),\n        )\n        structured = gemini.with_structured_output(DecomposedEntityList)\n\n        structure_prompt = (\n            \"Extract the medical entities from this analysis into a structured list. \"\n            \"Each entity needs a 'text' (the medical concept name) and 'entity_type' \"\n            \"(one of: Condition, Medication, Lab_Value, Procedure, Demographic, Other).\"\n            f\"\\n\\nAnalysis:\\n{raw_text}\"\n        )\n        gemini_model_name = os.getenv(\"GEMINI_MODEL_NAME\", \"gemini-2.5-flash\")\n\n        with llm_span(\"gemini_structure_medgemma_decompose\", gemini_model_name) as llm:\n            llm.set_request(structure_prompt)\n            result = await structured.ainvoke(structure_prompt)\n            llm.set_response(str(result))\n\n        if isinstance(result, dict):\n            result = DecomposedEntityList.model_validate(result)\n        decomposed = cast(DecomposedEntityList, result)\n\n        if decomposed.entities:\n            logger.info(\n                \"MedGemma decomposition found %d entities for '%s'\",\n                len(decomposed.entities),\n                normalized_text[:60],\n            )\n        else:\n            logger.warning(\n                \"MedGemma decomposition also returned 0 entities for '%s'\",\n                normalized_text[:80],\n            )\n\n        return [e.model_dump() for e in decomposed.entities]\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(\n            \"MedGemma entity decomposition failed for '%s': %s\",\n            criterion_text[:80],\n            e,\n            exc_info=True,\n        )\n        return []"
}

> AGENT

Both files are clean. Here's a summary of the changes:

**`entity_decomposer.py`** — Two additions:

1. **Retry with wording variations** (already in from before): When Gemini returns 0 entities, retries up to 2 times with slight rephrasing:
   - Retry 1: `"Eligibility criterion: {text}. Identify every medical concept."`
   - Retry 2: `"Clinical trial requirement — {text}. What medical entities are mentioned?"`

2. **New `medgemma_decompose_entities()` function**: When Gemini fails even after retries, asks MedGemma (the medical expert) to identify entities from the criterion text, then uses Gemini to structure MedGemma's free-text response into typed `DecomposedEntityList`.

**`parse.py`** — Replaced the blind "type everything as Condition" fallback:

- Collects indices where Gemini decomposition returned empty
- Runs `medgemma_decompose_entities()` concurrently for all those criteria
- Only skips a criterion if **both** Gemini (with retries) and MedGemma fail to extract any entities

**Flow for "Patients over 89 years old":**
1. Gemini attempt 0: returns empty → retry
2. Gemini attempt 1 (rephrased): returns empty → retry  
3. Gemini attempt 2 (rephrased): returns empty
4. MedGemma fallback: "age" → Demographic ✓

The services should auto-reload. Try re-uploading a protocol with simple age criteria to test.

> AGENT

The extraction uses `genai.Client` directly (Google AI Python SDK), not LangChain. For that one, the model name and API key are what matter — no explicit location needed since it uses the Google AI API.

For the `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` (LangChain) calls used in decomposition, grounding, and field mapping — these also use the Google AI API via `GOOGLE_API_KEY`. The `gemini-3-flash-preview` model should work if it's available via the Google AI API key.

> AGENT

The protocol-processor-service doesn't call `load_dotenv` — it relies on the env vars being set in the process environment. The api-service calls `load_dotenv(override=False)` at startup in `main.py`. Since the code reads `os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME")` at call time (not cached at import), the env var value at process start is what matters.

**You'll need to restart both services** for the new `REDACTED` to take effect. The `--reload` flag only watches for `.py` file changes, not `.env` changes.

Summary of changes:
- **`.env`**: `GEMINI_MODEL_NAME` changed from `gemini-2.5-flash` to `gemini-3-flash-preview`
- `GEMINI_LOCATION=global` was already set (though nothing reads it — the Google AI API key routes automatically)
- **entity_decomposer.py**: Retry logic (up to 2x with rephrased wording) + MedGemma fallback
- **parse.py**: MedGemma fallback replaces blind "type as Condition" fallback

After restarting the services, try uploading a protocol to test `gemini-3-flash-preview` with the retry+MedGemma decomposition flow.
